Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

and policy as negative for risk assets in the near-term

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

for US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is it still appropriate to have a positive stance on US 
high-yield (HY) bonds? 
Our view: We remain opportunistically bullish on US HY bonds. 
Lower sensitivity to interest rate volatility, a below-trend default 
rate and a maturity wall pushed out to 2028-29 drive this view. 
Rationale: Recent interest rate volatility has highlighted a key 
structural advantage of US HY bonds: their higher coupon rates  
and shorter tenors relative to Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds and US investment-grade (IG) corporate bonds translate into 
a much lower sensitivity to interest rates, cushioning bond prices 
during yield backup episodes, where bond yields rise sharply. 
YTD, US HY has delivered 1.8% in total returns, outperforming DM 
government bonds and US IG corporate bonds by over 3% each on 
a relative basis. This outperformance was particularly evident during 
the recent back-up in bond yields, as inflation concerns re-emerged 
after renewed Middle East tensions – an environment where HY's 
lower rate sensitivity provided a relative cushion  even as broader 
fixed income markets came under pressure in absolute terms. On 
the fundamentals side, US HY corporate credit quality remains 
resilient. Default rates continue to track below long-run trend levels, 
and the maturity wall has been pushed out to 2028 -29, reducing 
near-term refinancing risk. Against a non -recessionary macro 
backdrop, we expect these credit fundamentals to hold, supporting 
the case for continued opportunistic exposure to the asset class.  
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Do the latest BoJ and BoE meetings alter your view on the 
central banks’ rate paths and their corresponding currencies? 
Our view: We remain constructive on the JPY and expect two 
BoJ rate hikes this year and potentially one hike per quarter in 
H1 2027 as policy normalisation continues. Meanwhile, the BoE 
held rates at 3.75%. We expect GBP/USD to stay rangebound 
within 1.32-1.36 over the next 1-3 months, with downside risk 
towards the lower end of our medium-term 1.30-1.36 range. 
Rationale: Persistent inflation, higher energy prices and earlier JPY 
weakness support further BoJ policy normalisation . Continued 
hiking should progressively narrow the US -Japan rate gap, reduce 
the attractiveness of JPY -funded carry trades and support  the JPY 
over the medium term towards the 153-155 range. However, with a 
September hike largely priced  in, future tightening ’s pace  will be 
more important for the JPY. Meanwhile, the BoE was split 6-3 in its 
decision to hold rates at 3.75% , with three members preferring a 
25bps hike, signalling elevated inflation risks. However, a weaker 
labour market is likely to limit the pace of BoE tightening. The US-
UK rate divergence is expected to favour the USD over the next 1-3 
months. Further BoE tightening (if inflation remains elevated ) could 
narrow the rate gap and provide support to the GBP later in the cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

We expect US HY bonds to continue delivering 
strong performance in the near term

Document 186: Agent“重塑”服务业！Muse重燃“恐慌”，金融股“躺枪”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Agent“重塑”服务业！Muse重燃“恐慌”，金融股“躺枪”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

周⼆美股⾦融板块单⽇重挫约 2% 。摩根⼤通、富国银⾏、 Charles Schwab 等机构跌幅均
创数⽉新⾼。保险商 Allstate 、旅游预订平台 Booking Holdings 亦未能幸免。分析师指出，
AI 代理能⾃动完成⽐价、账单管理，直接⽡解依赖 " 消费者惯性 " 的商业模式。
摘要：
Meta 消费级 AI 代理 Muse 以压倒性下载量登顶苹果 App Store ，引发华尔街对 AI 颠覆⾦融服务业
的新⼀轮担忧，银⾏与券商股遭遇数⽉来最⼤单⽇跌幅。
9 ⽉ 22 ⽇，美股⾦融板块重挫，标普 500 ⾦融板块下跌约 2% ，拖累⼤盘基本收平。
券商嘉信理财与 LPL Financial 各跌逾 6% 。摩根⼤通下跌 3.4% ，创 7 ⽉以来最⼤单⽇跌幅；富国
银⾏跌 3.9% ，为 5 ⽉以来最⼤跌幅；美国银⾏与花旗分别下跌 3% 和 2% 。
保险商 Allstate 、旅游预订平台 Booking Holdings 亦未能幸免。
市场的核⼼担忧在于， Muse 此类 AI 智能体⼀旦⼤规模普及，将侵蚀那些依赖消费者⾏为惯性的

传统商业模式 ——从财富管理、经纪服务到保险定价，均⾯临被绕过甚⾄替代的⻛险。
分析⼈⼠指出，此次抛售的范围和烈度，已远超今年早些时候同类事件所引发的市场波动。
Muse 冲榜， “ 消费者惯性 ” 模式受到冲击
Muse 于 9 ⽉ 8 ⽇正式上线，发布后六天内下载量超过 90.2 万次，迅速登顶苹果美国区 iOS 及⾕歌
Play 免费应⽤榜⾸。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

该应⽤可代替⽤⼾完成数字化任务，通过接⼊ Gmail 、 OpenTable 等第三⽅服务实现跨平台操
作，并已与 PayPal 及⾦融科技公司 Plaid 建⽴合作。
Citizens ⾦融服务与⾦融科技研究主管 Devin Ryan 指出，尽管没有哪款 AI 代理能在⼀夜之间颠覆
整个⾏业，但近⼏个⽉密集的产品发布 " 让市场形成了⼀种叙事 —— 世界正在改变，不确定性随
之上升 " 。
AI 代理对⾦融机构的威胁，远不⽌于替代⼈⼯顾问或分流渠道保费。它的真正颠覆性在于，能
够替⽤⼾⾃动完成⽐价、切换服务和管理账单。这直接摧毁了依赖 “ 消费者惯性 ” ⽣存的商业模
式—— ⽤⼾在缺乏主动决策时倾向于维持现有消费习惯。
⾼盛交易台在研究报告中指出，随着 AI 代理在价格⽐较、出⾏预订和客服交互上的能⼒持续提
升，电信、保险、公⽤事业等依赖周期性账单、可协商定价和附加服务的⾏业将率先承压。
周⼆⾼盛编制的 " 消费者惯性 " ⻛险股票篮⼦单⽇下跌 2.6% ，为近六个⽉最差表现，过去六个交

易⽇累计跌幅已超 7% 。⾼盛⻛险篮⼦涵盖 AT&T 、 T-Mobile 、 Allstate 、 Progressive 、 Netflix 、
Paramount Skydance 及 Expedia 、 Booking 等标的。
Wayve 资管⾸席策略师 Rhys Williams 表⽰：
券商与财富管理⾸当其冲
在此次抛售中，券商股跌幅更甚。嘉信理财跌逾 6% ， LPL Financial 下挫逾 7% ， Raymond
James 和 Ameriprise Financial 亦出现明显下跌。
Citizens ⾦融服务与⾦融科技研究主管 Devin Ryan 解释称，威胁不仅限于 AI 替代⼈⼯顾问、压缩
收费空间这⼀层⾯。
在他看来，智能体有可能更⾼效地替客⼾完成税收损失收割等资⾦调配操作，从⽽减少资⾦在
经纪账⼾中的沉淀时间，压缩这些公司将客⼾闲置资⾦转化为⾃⾝收益的空间。Ryan 表⽰：
值得注意的是，此次恐慌并⾮⾸次。今年早些时候，⾦融科技公司 Altruist 推出个性化税务策略

⼯具时，曾引发类似抛售；今年 2 ⽉ Anthropic 发布 Claude 代理⼯具后，软件即服务类股票亦曾
遭遇⼀轮暴跌。
但分析⼈⼠认为，凭借 Meta 旗下各应⽤数⼗亿级的现有⽤⼾基础， Muse 的潜在影响范围更⼴、
渗透速度更快。
银⾏股承压，基本⾯隐忧叠加
除了 AI 代理带来的冲击外，银⾏业⾃⾝基本⾯也⾯临考验。
Muse 毫⽆疑问对这类公司是负⾯因素。现在它还更像是⼀个新奇事物，但我认为两年后我们都
会在使⽤代理。
如果⼀个智能体全天候优化资产配置，那么系统中的沉淀现⾦余额是否会被消耗殆尽？

市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂

华尔街⻅闻提及，美国银⾏ CEO Brian Moynihan 表⽰，第三季度销售与交易收⼊ " 将是我们有史
以来较好的三季度之⼀，但与去年相⽐将基本持平 " 。
富国银⾏⾸席财务官 Mike Santomassimo 上周在巴克莱举办的⾦融业会议上表⽰，贷款等核⼼
业务增⻓将持续，但增速料较上半年有所放缓。上述表态已在上周引发银⾏股更⼤范围的回
调。
⽽美联储加息周期的背景下，收益率曲线趋平进⼀步压缩了银⾏的息差空间。
多⽅观点：颠覆是机遇，也是威胁
⾯对市场恐慌，部分投资者和咨询顾问提醒不宜过度悲观。
财富管理咨询机构 Pirker Partners 创始⼈ Alois Pirker 表⽰，⼤型⾦融机构掌握着丰富的客⼾数
据，本质上具备技术转型的先天优势， " 这对这些机构⽽⾔是⼀个巨⼤的机遇 " ，但转型需要时

间， " 毕竟你不能让⼀艘远洋邮轮在⼀夜之间掉头 " 。
上周， Anthropic 刚刚推出⼀套 Claude ⼯作流⼯具，已接⼊ Schwab 和 Vanguard 等平台。 Gabelli
Funds 投资组合经理 Macrae Sykes 在邮件中写道， " 若 Schwab 能够以此服务顾问、提升其建议
质量和资产获取效率，最终将通过托管、客⼾互动等环节使公司受益 " 。
⾼盛策略师的判断则更为审慎： " 这仍然⽐ ' 买⼊⼀切 AI' 要复杂微妙得多。 "
随着 Anthropic 和 OpenAI 相继推出更低成本的前沿模型， AI 的下⼀波浪潮或不仅仅是少数实验室
构建超⼤规模算⼒集群，⽽是由数⼗亿分散的智能体共同推动。
这将重塑整个 AI 基础设施需求的格局，⽽⾦融业将在这⼀进程中持续承压与调适。

势。

### Page 1

纳斯达克 100 指数突破 6 ⽉初的前⾼纪录，存储板块领涨，闪迪单⽇暴涨 6.8% ，市场正将
NAND 正从传统存储产品重新定位为 "AI 基础设施中更具系统关键性的组件 " 。经历 6 ⽉去杠

摘要：
AI 交易强势回归，叠加盈利预期持续上修，纳斯达克 100 指数周⼆收于历史新⾼，存储芯⽚板块
领涨全场， NAND 闪存正从⼤宗商品向 AI 基础设施核⼼组件加速转型。
Meta 旗下 Muse AI 模型上线后迅速登顶苹果应⽤商店免费榜⾸，点燃市场对 AI 需求的乐观预期。
纳斯达克 100 指数周⼆收涨 0.8% ，创下历史新⾼，并在盘中突破 6 ⽉初的前⾼纪录；纳斯达克综
合指数则连续两⽇刷新峰值，周⼀单⽇涨幅达 2.3% ，为七周最⼤。与此同时，标普 500 指数⼩
幅收跌，市场分化格局凸显。
存储板块成为本轮⾏情最⼤赢家。 Sandisk 单⽇⼤涨 6.8% ， Seagate Technology 涨 4.8% ，

Western Digital 涨 3.7% 。油价回落与美中 AI 谈判进展的报道，进⼀步为科技股提供了上⾏动
⼒。
FOMO 情绪重燃，科技股 “ 全员回池 ”
本轮反弹的核⼼驱动⼒，是市场对 AI 交易的重新定价。巴克莱⾸席欧洲股票策略师 Emmanuel
Cau 表⽰，“AI FOMO （错失恐惧）正以强劲之势卷⼟重来 ” ，并指出此次上涨背后有 “ 多重因素
共同作⽤ ” ，包括油价下跌以及美中 AI 谈判的相关报道。他同时强调，经历 6 ⽉去杠杆之后，科
技板块的持仓结构已 “ 明显更为⼲净 ” ，此次上涨正是在此基础上展开的。
Visdom Investment Group 联席交易主管 Michael Zigmont 将周⼀的交易描述为 “ 所有⼈重新跳⼊
泳池的时刻 ” ，认为当前⾛势 “ 看起来像是科技股新⼀轮动量⾏情的起点 ” 。
Bespoke Investment Group 的数据显⽰，当纳斯达克综合指数单⽇涨幅超过 2% 并同步创出新⾼
时，此后⼀个⽉的平均涨幅历史上达到 3.7% ，三个⽉、六个⽉及⼀年维度的回报同样⾼于均

值。周⼆纳指再度收涨 0.5% ，进⼀步印证了这⼀历史规律。

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
盈利预期上修，估值重估窗⼝打开
⽀撑本轮⾏情的不仅是情绪⾯，基本⾯同样在提供⽀撑。 Edward Jones 投资策略主管 Mona
Mahajan 表⽰， AI 交易 " 明年很可能仍有持续动⼒ " ，背后是 AI 基础设施建设的巨额资本⽀出，⽽
AI 在⽣产⼒层⾯的⼴泛渗透将进⼀步拓宽受益范围。
Yardeni Research 在研报中指出， " 投资者已注意到分析师上调盈利预期的速度快于股价涨幅 " ，
这可能促使市场重新审视部分⼤型科技股及半导体股的估值。
Mahajan 还表⽰，美联储本⽉的加息更多属于政策利率的 " 周期中段调整 " ，⽽⾮激进紧缩周期的
开启，叠加亚特兰⼤联储 GDPNow 模型对三季度实际 GDP 年化增速 5.1% 的预测（较⼆季度的
1.5% ⼤幅跃升），企业盈利扩张的宏观基础依然稳固。三季度财报季即将开启，有望成为下⼀个

催化剂。
NAND 重新定价，存储迈向关键组件
存储板块的强势表现，折射出市场对 NAND 闪存战略价值的重新认知。 Rosenblatt 分析师 Kevin
Cassidy 周⼀发布研报，⾸次覆盖闪迪并给予买⼊评级，核⼼论点是：AI 系统正在 " 创造机会 " ，
将 NAND 从传统商品存储产品重新定位为 "AI 基础设施中更具系统关键性的组件 " 。
Cassidy 指出，传统 NAND 需求由密度提升和单位成本下降驱动，但⼤型 AI 模型及推理⼯作负
载 " 越来越优先考虑密度、性能、耐久性和供应确定性，⽽⾮最低绝对价格 " 。这⼀需求结构的转
变，从根本上改变了 NAND 的定价逻辑。
在技术路线上，闪迪与⽇本铠侠联合开发的 BiCS8 及 BiCS10 架构通过垂直堆叠存储单元实现更
⾼密度， Cassidy 认为这将⽀撑闪迪在⼤规模 AI 存储市场保持 " 性能与成本的双重优势 " 。他预计
闪迪 2027 财年数据中⼼营收将达 217 亿美元， 2028 财年进⼀步增⾄ 286 亿美元，并给出 2400 美

元的⽬标价，较周⼆收盘价 1887.04 美元隐含约 30% 的上⾏空间。
Cassidy 还特别提及闪迪已与⼋家主要 NAND 客⼾签署多年期商业协议，允许客⼾以锁定价格保
障供应。他认为，这类协议覆盖闪迪⼤部分产能， " 有望提升需求能⻅度，并降低历史上 NAND
周期的波动性 " ，从⽽为公司估值提供更稳定的⽀撑。

Document 196: AI替代潮被高估？巴克莱：仅20%职场核心技能可被高度复制，但放大效应已悄然蔓延

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI替代潮被高估？巴克莱：仅20%职场核心技能可被高度复制，但放大效应已悄然蔓延.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

AI 对劳动⼒的冲击，⽐想象中更温和，也更深远。巴克莱最新研究打破 " 替代潮 " 恐慌 —— 真
正能被 AI 复制的职场核⼼技能仅约 20% ，但其 " 放⼤效应 " 却⼏乎渗透每个职业⻆落。更值得
警惕的是： AI 技能需求正从科技岗向管理层蔓延，⽽物理 AI 的崛起，甚⾄可能让⼈类专业
技能成为可规模化授权的新型知识产权。
摘要：
AI 对劳动⼒市场的冲击或许远⽐市场叙事更为温和，但也更为深远。巴克莱最新研究显⽰，真正
能被 AI ⾼度复制的职场核⼼技能仅占约 20% ，⼤规模 " 替代潮 " 的担忧存在⾼估；然⽽ AI 对⽣产率
的 " 放⼤效应 " 却呈现出截然不同的图景 —— 其覆盖⾯远⽐⾃动化替代宽⼴，⼏乎渗透⾄劳动⼒市
场的每个⻆落。
巴克莱于 9 ⽉ 22 ⽇发布报告，推出 "AI ⾃动化与放⼤框架 " （ Barclays 3A Framework ），以技能⽽
⾮职业为核⼼分析单元，系统评估认知型 AI 与物理型 AI 对逾 830 个职业的影响。研究发现，⾃动
化⻛险⾼度集中于少数职业群体，认知型 AI 的⾼度暴露职业主要集中在计算机、⼯程和科学类

岗位，物理型 AI 的⾼度暴露职业则集中在建筑、⽣产、安装、维护与维修领域。与此同时，美
国招聘数据已出现与 AI 相符的结构性变化 —— ⾃ 2022 年以来，⾃动化暴露度最⾼职业的招聘职
位占⽐已从约 30% 回落⾄ 25% 左右。
AI 技能需求的急速扩张则揭⽰出另⼀重逻辑：在美国、英国、法国和德国，要求具备 AI 技能的职
位占⽐已从 2019 年的约 2% 攀升⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉的 6% ⾄ 10% 。值得注意的是，AI 技能需求的重⼼
正在发⽣漂移 ——2019 年逾 70% 的 AI 相关职位集中于科技与数据岗位，⾄ 2026 年这⼀⽐例已降
⾄约 50% ，⽽管理类职位的占⽐则从约 10% 升⾄ 25% ，显⽰ AI 正从专业技术领域向更⼴泛的职
场⽣态扩散。
AI ⾃动化或放⼤的是具体技能⽽⾮整个职业本⾝
传统的 AI 劳动⼒冲击分析多以 " 职业 " 为单位，⽽巴克莱认为，职业是任务的集合，任务⼜是技能
的集合，技能才是理解 AI 影响的 " 原⼦单元 " 。 AI 更有可能⾃动化或放⼤的是具体技能 —— 如信息

收集、写作或数据分析 —— ⽽⾮整个职业本⾝。
3A 框架基于美国劳⼯部数据库，该数据库涵盖 104 项跨职业所需技能，并将其映射⾄逾 800 个职
业。框架创新体现在三个维度：其⼀，同时纳⼊认知型 AI 和物理型 AI ，涵盖数字智能与实体智能
两类形态；其⼆，以技能取代任务或职业作为基本分析单位；其三，同时捕捉 " ⾃动化 " 与 " 放
⼤ " 两种效应 —— 前者衡量 AI 对⼈类劳动的替代程度，后者衡量 AI 提升⼈类技能⽣产率的潜⼒。
研究结果显⽰，仅约 20% 的职场技能被评定为 AI ⾼度可复制， 40% 的技能对认知型 AI 呈现较强抗
性，对物理型 AI 抗性更强的技能⽐例则⾼达 70% ，表明物理型 AI ⽬前的⾃动化影响范围仍远窄于
认知型 AI 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⾃动化暴露⾼度集中，⼤部分劳动⼒相对安全
在⾃动化暴露分布上，巴克莱的关键发现是：极⾼暴露程度仅集中于极少数职业，⼤多数职业
主要偏向于对认知型 AI 或物理型 AI 的⼀种暴露，⽽⾮两者兼具。
认知型 AI ⾃动化暴露最⾼的职业包括数据科学家、统计学家和精算师，其核⼼技能 —— 信息检
索、模式识别、⽂档处理与程序化决策 —— 与 AI 能⼒⾼度重合。物理型 AI 暴露最⾼的则是农业⼯
⼈、纺织机器操作员、结构钢铁⼯⼈等依赖重复性体⼒劳动的职业。
相⽐之下，约 120 个职业对两类 AI ⾃动化的综合暴露均相对较低，覆盖约 20% 的美国劳动⼒，主
要包括教师、⼉童看护⼈员、教练及部分酒店与娱乐从业者 —— 这些职业⾼度依赖⼈际互动、
现场监督或娱乐表演，难以被 AI 复制。

### Page 3

放⼤效应：覆盖更⼴，潜在收益更⼤
与⾃动化的集中分布不同， AI 的放⼤效应呈现出更加⼴泛和均匀的特征。在巴克莱的模型中，放
⼤效应在可复制技能、部分可复制技能与不可复制技能⽐例最为均衡的职业中最为显著 ——AI
承接其中的可复制部分，同时提升剩余⼈类技能的价值与产出效率。
认知型 AI 放⼤效应最⾼的职业包括空中交通管制员、医疗健康服务经理及培训与发展经理等管理
类职位， AI 可接管⾏政⽂档与信息处理⼯作，同时放⼤领导、辅导和⼈员管理等⼈类核⼼能⼒。
物理型 AI 放⼤效应则在多类机械与技术⼈员职业中最为突出，例如公共汽⻋和卡⻋机械师、⼯业
机械师等，部分例⾏体⼒作业可被⾃动化，⽽诊断、决策和应变能⼒仍需⼈类主导。
巴克莱指出，放⼤效应分布的核⼼信息在于： AI 的⾃动化能⼒集中于特定职业，⽽其提升⽣产率
的能⼒则覆盖极为⼴泛的职业群体，医⽣、教师和⼯程师等迥异职业均可从 AI ⼯具中实质性获
益。这⼀特征意味着， AI 的整体⽣产率增益在⻓期可能超过其直接替代效应。
招聘数据印证结构转变， AI 技能需求向管理层扩散

劳动⼒市场数据已开始与上述框架形成印证。据巴克莱对 LinkUp 招聘数据的分析，⾃ 2022 年以
来，⾃动化暴露度最⾼职业的职位空缺占⽐从接近 30% 降⾄约 25% ，其中软件⼯程岗位的下滑
贡献最为突出；⾃动化暴露度最低职业的职位占⽐则从约 20% ⼩幅上升⾄ 21% ⾄ 23% 。
与此同时，专项 AI 技能需求正在加速扩张。据 Indeed Hiring Lab 等基于更⼴泛关键词搜索的数
据，美国、英国、法国和德国的 AI 相关职位占⽐，已从 2019 年的约 2% 分别升⾄ 2026 年的 6% ⾄
10% 不等，英国升幅最为显著。
更具结构性意义的是需求主体的转变。 2019 年，科技与数据岗位主导逾 70% 的 AI 相关职位发
布；⾄ 2026 年，这⼀占⽐已降⾄约 50% ，⽽管理类职位占⽐则从约 10% 升⾄约 25% 。这⼀趋势
在美国、英国、法国和德国均有体现，表明 AI 正以普适⼯作技能⽽⾮专属技术的形态向整个职场
渗透。
物理 AI 崛起：技能或成可授权的知识产权

报告进⼀步前瞻了物理型 AI 普及所催⽣的新型经济模式，并将其划分为两个阶段。
在第⼀阶段，⼈类充当 " 机器⼈训练师 "—— 由于物理型 AI 缺乏等同于⼤语⾔模型训练语料的现实
世界数据集，⾏业正通过远程操控、第⼀视⻆视频采集等⽅式，依赖⼈类⽰范为机器⼈⽣成训
练数据。 Figure's Index 、 Scale AI 等公司已在构建这⼀众包⽣态。
在第⼆阶段，价值重⼼将向 " 操作规程 " 本⾝转移。随着物理型 AI 成熟，焦点将从硬件转向内嵌其
中的技能与⼯作流程 —— 焊接、⼿术、烹饪等⼈类专业知识，有望被编码为可规模化授权的 " 机

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

### Page 1

N

BÁO CÁO NGẮN CƠ HỘI ĐẦU TƯ

15/09/2026

CTCP Nhựa Tiền Phong (NTP) 
Tăng tốc trong chu kỳ thuận lợi

Mua 
Giá mục tiêu VND 73.200 
Vùng hỗ trợ/Điểm mua 1 50.000 – 51.400 
Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng 1 44% 
Vùng hỗ trợ/Điểm mua 2 42.000 – 44.000 
Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng 2 70%

Lưu ý: 
Nhà đầu tư cần kết hợp nhận định xu hướng thị 
trường chung, mức độ hấp dẫn của các điểm 
nhấn đầu tư và khẩu vị rủi ro của mình để quản 
lý tỷ trọng tại từng điểm mua cho phù hợp. 
Do việc xác định mỗi điểm mua được dựa trên 
các khung thời gian khác nhau nên chúng tôi 
không đưa ra mức dừng lỗ cố định. Nhìn chung, 
mức dừng lỗ nên được đặt chặt hơn cho điểm 
mua gần (5-7%) và rộng hơn cho điểm mua xa 
(10-15%).

ĐIỂM NHẤN ĐẦU TƯ 
Kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ tăng trưởng mạnh hơn so với giai đoạn trước. KBSV 
dự báo sản lượng tiêu thụ của NTP tăng 2,3% CAGR giai đoạn 2026 - 2028 nhờ (1) 
Đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư hạ tầng tại miền Bắc (2) gia tăng đầu tư hạ tầng tại các 
địa phương thu hút FDI (3) tăng tốc đơn hàng mảng dự án.

Giá hạt nhựa sẽ gây áp lực biên lợi nhuận nửa cuối 2026 nhưng không làm thay 
đổi triển vọng dài hạn. Mặc dù duy trì chính sách dự phòng nguyên liệu cao và đa 
dạng hóa nhà cung cấp, nhưng NTP vẫn đối mặt với rủi ro thu hẹp lợi nhuận, 
nhưng sẽ không làm giảm đáng kể triển vọng tăng trưởng trung hạn. KBSV cho 
rằng tình trạng dư cung hạt nhựa tại Trung Quốc vẫn sẽ duy trì trong trung hạn và 
xung đột được giải quyết, giảm áp lực biên lợi nhuận toàn ngành.

Catalyst thoái vốn kỳ vọng tái định giá doanh nghiệp. Theo kế hoạch thoái vốn 
Nhà nước, SCIC dự kiến thoái toàn bộ 37,1% cổ phần tại NTP. Chúng tôi đánh giá 
việc hiện thực hóa thương vụ có thể giúp NTP (1) thu hút nhà đầu tư chiến lược 
mới; (2) cộng hưởng mô hình kinh doanh; (3) tái định giá doanh nghiệp.

Hoạt động kinh doanh  Diễn biến doanh thu và lợi nhuận thuần 5 năm gần nhất

NTP là một trong những doanh nghiệp đầu 
ngành ống nhựa tại Việt Nam, với thị phần 
khoảng 31% toàn quốc và 60% tại miền Bắc. 
Hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi tập trung vào 
sản xuất và phân phối ống nhựa cùng các sản 
phẩm phụ tùng. Nhờ lợi thế về thương hiệu, 
quy mô và mạng lưới phân phối, NTP duy trì 
vị thế dẫn đầu và nền tảng tăng trưởng ổn 
định qua các năm.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KB Securities Vietnam

Tương quan mặt bằng giá  Diễn biến P/E của doanh nghiệp

NTP đang được giao dịch với mức P/E khoảng 
8,3 lần thấp hơn trung bình 5 năm của cổ 
phiếu. Với kỳ vọng doanh nghiệp sẽ bước vào 
pha tăng trưởng mạnh trong 3 năm tới, chúng 
tôi cho rằng P/E fw 2026F ở mức 9,5x lần phù 
hợp với mức dự phóng tăng trưởng 11,6% 
CAGR giai đoạn 2026 – 2028.

1. Kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ tăng trưởng mạnh hơn so với giai đoạn 
trước do nhu cầu tăng cao tại thị trường miền Bắc 
Các dự án hạ tầng đang triển khai 
đồng bộ tại miền Bắc

Miền Bắc đang bước vào một chu kỳ đầu tư hạ tầng mới, đặt nền tảng thuận lợi 
cho hoạt động xây dựng, công nghiệp và đô thị trong trung hạn, hàng loạt dự án 
liên vùng quy mô lớn như Vành đai 4 Vùng Thủ đô, tuyến đường sắt Lào Cai – Hà 
Nội – Hải Phòng và các trục Hà Nội – Hải Phòng – Quảng Ninh, qua đó từng bước 
hoàn thiện mạng lưới giao thông chiến lược và nâng cao năng lực liên kết giữa 
các trung tâm kinh tế lớn tại khu vực. Chúng tôi nhận thấy tiến độ triển khai các 
dự án trên có thể khác biệt so với các dự án hạ tầng trong quá khứ về năng lực 
triển khai nhờ (1) thúc đẩy giải ngân đầu tư công, ưu tiên nguồn lực cho các công 
trình trọng điểm và có tính lan tỏa cao; (2) quyết tâm lớn hơn của Chính phủ 
trong việc xử lý các vướng mắc về thủ tục, giải phóng mặt bằng và phân bổ vốn; 
và (3) yêu cầu cao từ việc nâng cao năng lực vận tải, logistics và kết nối giữa Hà 
Nội với các trung tâm sản xuất, khu công nghiệp và cảng biển phía Bắc.

Khi các tuyến giao thông chiến lược dần hoàn thiện, khả năng tiếp cận các khu 
vực vệ tinh được cải thiện sẽ hỗ trợ khai thác quỹ đất mới và thu hút thêm dòng 
vốn vào bất động sản, khu công nghiệp và logistics. Về mặt dài hạn, việc đầu tư 
hiện tại mang lợi ích kép do (1) gia tăng giá trị xây dựng trực tiếp; (2)  mở rộng 
không gian kinh tế, thúc đẩy đầu tư tư nhân và đặt nền tảng phát triển dài hạn.

các trung tâm công nghiệp lớn phía Bắc. Bên cạnh xu hướng đa dạng hóa chuỗi

3

cung ứng theo chiến lược China +1, chúng tôi cho rằng sức hút FDI của khu vực 
bao gồm (1) hệ sinh thái sản xuất điện tử và công nghệ cao đã hình thành tương 
đối hoàn chỉnh, tạo hiệu ứng thu hút các nhà cung cấp và doanh nghiệp phụ trợ; 
(2) vị trí thuận lợi trong kết nối với chuỗi cung ứng Trung Quốc và các cảng biển 
lớn phía Bắc; và (3) mạng lưới khu công nghiệp, giao thông và logistics ngày 
càng được hoàn thiện, giúp cải thiện khả năng triển khai và mở rộng hoạt động 
sản xuất.

Ngoài ra, dòng vốn mới đang có xu hướng tập trung nhiều hơn vào các lĩnh vực 
có giá trị gia tăng cao như điện tử, bán dẫn và sản xuất công nghệ cao. Chúng tôi 
kỳ vọng sự mở rộng của các cụm sản xuất này sẽ tạo hiệu ứng lan tỏa sang hoạt 
động đầu tư nhà máy, khu công nghiệp, logistics và phát triển đô thị tại các khu 
vực vệ tinh. FDI không chỉ đóng góp trực tiếp vào năng lực sản xuất mà còn tiếp 
tục là một trong những động lực hỗ trợ tăng trưởng đầu tư và xây dựng tại miền 
Bắc.

Biểu đồ 5. FDI đăng ký theo miền  Biểu đồ 6. Sản lượng tiêu thụ của NTP

Nguồn: Fiinpro, KBSV   Nguồn: NTP, KBSV

Kênh dự án được kỳ vọng đóng góp 
đáng kể vào doanh thu giai đoạn 
2026–2028 
 Kênh dự án sẽ là động lực thúc đẩy sản lượng của NTP trong giai đoạn 2026–
2028 nhờ (1) giá trị các dự án đã được cập nhật và dự kiến triển khai trong năm 
2026 đạt gần 1.750 tỷ đồng, tương đương khoảng 24% kế hoạch doanh thu, đóng 
góp đáng kể vào tăng trưởng doanh thu; (2) NTP đang tăng cường nguồn lực cho 
các Ban Phát triển thị trường và duy trì chính sách giá cạnh tranh nhằm nâng tỷ 
lệ tiếp cận, trúng dự án và mở rộng quy mô đơn hàng.

Chúng tôi dự báo sản lượng tiêu thụ của NTP trong 2026/2027 lần lượt đạt 
150/153 nghìn tấn, tương ứng tăng 12%/2% svck, với tỷ lệ sử dụng công suất cao 
nhất có thể đạt khoảng 72%. Với quan điểm tích cực về sự hưởng lợi của doanh 
nghiệp nhờ thúc đẩy hạ tầng, tăng tỷ lệ đô thị hóa và phát triển tại miền Bắc và 
hợp tác với các dự án xây dựng sẽ giúp NTP đạt được mức sản lượng tiêu thụ ở 
nền mới so với giai đoạn trước.

1.  2. Biên lợi nhuận gộp có thể chịu áp lực trong nửa cuối 2026 nhưng 
không làm thay đổi triển vọng dài hạn.  
Đa dạng hóa nguồn nhập khẩu giúp 
hạn chế rủi ro gián đoạn nguyên liệu

Nhựa Tiền Phong thường tăng cường nhập nguyên liệu khi giá đầu vào ở mức 
thấp, để đảm bảo hoạt động liên tục và duy trì số ngày tồn kho bình quân 
khoảng 96 ngày, chiếm 31% COGS, nhờ vậy NTP luôn có độ trễ so với biến động 
giá thị trường và chi phí thực tế ghi nhận vào giá vốn. Tuy nhiên, áp lực chi phí có 
thể phản ánh trong các quý sau nếu giá nguyên liệu duy trì ở mức cao trong thời 
gian dài.

Tăng giá chậm hơn thị trường để gia 
tăng thị phần  
 Trong 2Q2026, giá hạt nhựa PVC vẫn tiếp diễn đà tăng từ Q1/2026, khoảng 6,5% 
svck (từ 646 lên 688 USD/tấn), trong khi NTP giữ nguyên mức tăng giá bán kể từ 
đầu năm, thấp hơn mức điều chỉnh của các đối thủ trong ngành.

Về mặt chiến lược, KBSV cho rằng đây là sự đánh đổi lợi ích ngắn hạn để gia tăng 
thị phần. Việc duy trì mức giá cạnh tranh giúp NTP hạn chế áp lực lên hệ thống 
phân phối, giữ chân khách hàng và củng cố thị phần trong giai đoạn chi phí 
nguyên liệu tăng cao. Nhờ đó, sản lượng tiêu thụ 2Q2026 ước tính tăng 17% svck. 
Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận sẽ tạo đáy tại Q3/2026 nhờ (1) giá bán niêm yết 
duy trì ở nền cao (2) giá hạt nhựa hạ nhiệt.

3. Catalyst thoái vốn kỳ vọng tái định giá doanh nghiệp 
  SCIC dự kiến thoái toàn bộ 37,1% cổ phần tại NTP, sau nhiều năm có tên trong 
danh sách các doanh nghiệp dự kiến giảm tỷ lệ sở hữu nhà nước. KBSV kỳ vọng 
mức định giá sẽ năm ở mức cao do (1) doanh nghiệp dẫn đầu thị trường ống 
nhựa miền Bắc; (2) khả năng cộng hưởng đáng kể về nguyên liệu, công nghệ và 
hệ thống phân phối đối với nhà đầu tư chiến lược; (3) tài chính lành mạnh với 
mức cổ tức tiền mặt hấp dẫn. Việc thoái vốn có thể là chất xúc tác trong ngắn 
hạn cho đà tăng của giá cổ phiếu.

trung hạn gây áp lực tới biên lợi 
nhuận  
 Với chính sách duy trì tồn kho nguyên liệu ở mức cao, tương đương khoảng 3–9 
tháng nhập khẩu, NTP có độ trễ nhất định trong việc phản ánh biến động giá 
hạt nhựa vào giá vốn. Trong trường hợp giá nguyên liệu tăng, lượng tồn kho giá 
thấp có thể giúp duy trì biên lợi nhuận trong ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên, nếu mặt 
bằng giá cao kéo dài vượt quá chu kỳ tồn kho, áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận sẽ phụ 
thuộc đáng kể vào khả năng điều chỉnh giá bán của NTP. Việc tăng giá bán có 
thể giúp bù đắp một phần chi phí đầu vào, nhưng mức độ và thời điểm điều 
chỉnh còn phụ thuộc vào diễn biến cạnh tranh và chính sách chiết khấu trên thị 
trường.

Nhu cầu tiêu thụ chậm hơn dự kiến

Tăng trưởng sản lượng dự báo của NTP phụ thuộc nhiều vào tiến độ giải ngân 
đầu tư công, dòng vốn FDI, xây dựng dân dụng và kênh dự án. Rủi ro đến từ việc 
chậm giải ngân và bối cảnh vĩ mô không thuận lợi là hai yếu tố trọng yếu tác 
động tiêu cực tới nhu cầu tiêu thụ.

Document 240: Eight Reasons Why the Fed Should Raise Rates (2026-09-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eight Reasons Why the Fed Should Raise Rates (2026-09-14).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.33 / 14 September 2026EDITORIAL
CHART 1
THE EIGHT REASONS WHY THE FED SHOULD RAISE RATES
1 U.S. productivity gains are significant but attributing them to the AI boom is
premature: see our Chart of the Week from May 27, 2026, United States: Labour
productivity growth set to continue.
The FOMC meeting on September 15–16 is expected to mark a turning point with the Fed’s first rate hike since May 2023.

While there may have been economic reasons to hold off and maintain the status quo until now (some negative signals
on the employment front and some encouraging ones on the inflation front), the conditions for a necessary recalibration
now appear to be in place.
Between the start of the year and September 11, out of a sample
of nearly 90 central banks worldwide, 25 lowered their policy rates
(an average cumulative decrease of 85 basis points), 25 raised them

(an average cumulative increase of 75 basis points), and 36 left them
unchanged. The U.S. Federal Reserve is part of this status quo group,
while the ECB is among the central banks that have raised their rates
(twice). Which is the odd one, the Fed or the ECB? At first glance, the
Fed’s status quo seems harder to explain than the ECB’s rate hike:
U.S. growth and inflation are significantly higher than Eurozone me -
trics. Why would this situation warrant a rate hike in the latter case but

not (yet) in the former? In short, because the Fed has a dual mandate
and, until recently, its focus has been more on the maximum employ -
ment component than on price stability.
Will the Fed continue to diverge from its peers this week, given that
the ECB implemented another rate hike on September 10 th and that
further tightening by the BoJ (September 17 th–18th) is also widely an -
ticipated? And while a status quo from the BoE (which will also meet

on September 17th) is expected, the vote is likely to be close (6-3 ac -
cording to our forecasts, possibly 6-4) and the tone should be hawkish.
In our new base case scenario, we believe the Fed will join the ranks
of the hikers this week: it would no longer wait until December 2026
to begin the (short) cycle of (three) rate hikes (25 bp each) that we
have been anticipating for several months. However, the outcome of
the September 15–16 FOMC meeting remains somewhat uncertain: we

believe the conditions are in place for a monetary policy adjustment.
But will the Fed share the same view?
WHAT HAS HELD THE FED BACK FROM RAISING RATES SO FAR? SOME SIGNS
OF WEAKNESS IN THE LABOUR MARKET AND THE K-SHAPED NATURE OF
GROWTH
This latter is running at a relatively high pace (an average of 2.2% year-
over-year since Q1 2025, double the Eurozone growth rate) but rests
on narrow foundations that are largely dependent on the AI boom. It is

this sector that is driving a large portion of non-residential investment
(with, conversely, equally significant imports). And it is the wealth ef -
fects stemming from the sharp rise in stock market indices—driven by
tech stocks—that largely underpin consumption among the wealthiest
households, while consumption among lower-income households is
struggling, constrained, and affected by the high cost of living.
Strong on the surface but more fragile beneath: this characteristic of

U.S. growth has likely contributed, thus far, to tipping the scales in fa-
vor of the monetary status quo. The aim was to avoid the risk of stalling
the AI engine and exacerbating the financial difficulties of lower-inco -
me households. An idea has also circulated: that this strong growth,
driven by productivity gains (supposedly resulting from the deploy -
ment of AI1), might be disinflationary rather than inflationary.
This theory has not, for the time being, been fully validated by the data:

on the contrary, the AI boom appears to be rather inflationary in the
short term, which should lead the Fed to reconsider its status quo.
The labour market, like economic growth, has both a positive side and a
more worrisome one. The first is characterized by a low unemployment
rate, downwardly oriented over the past few months, a trend which is
expected to continue. The second stems from the volatility and relative
weakness of job gains through July, the downward pressures on labour

supply, and the uncomfortable balance of a situation combining low firing
and low hiring. The Fed has been more sensitive to these vulnerabilities,
in line with its dual mandate.
HIGH INFLATION — THOUGH TRENDING DOWNWARD ACCORDING TO CER-
TAIN ALTERNATIVE MEASURES — AND ANCHORED INFLATION EXPECTA-
TIONS HAVE ALSO HELPED THE FED ADOPT A WAIT-AND-SEE APPROACH.
U.S. inflation is high and has been well above the 2% target for now
six years, according to the most widely used metrics; while alternative

measures are closer to the target (see Chart 1). The trend, drivers, and
composition of inflation also matter. As for its trend, data from June
and July showed a slight downward trend, an encouraging sign further
supported by the stability (no increase) seen in August (3.4% year-on-
year for the headline CPI, with core inflation even edging down slightly
from 2.5% to 2.4% y/y).

### Page 2

4
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.33 / 14 September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
On the other hand, if we compare inflation with the trend in unit labor
costs—a key driver, as Richard Clarida (former Fed vice chair) 2 points
out—it clearly appears to be trending downward, with further disinfla-
tion on the horizon (see Chart 2).
Finally, in terms of composition, current inflationary pressures stem
largely from the energy supply shock caused by the conflict in Iran.

Such a development does not require an immediate response from
the central bank 3, as long as inflation expectations remain anchored
(which is currently the case) and provided that the inflation spike re -
mains transitory, does not become widespread, and does not trigger
second-round effects (which cannot be taken entirely for granted over
the next few months, given persistent upward pressure on energy
prices combined with resilient U.S. growth).

WHY IS THE MONETARY STATUS QUO NO LONGER TENABLE? THERE ARE AT
LEAST SEVEN REASONS
A combination of factors justifies the Fed raising its policy rates, star -
ting in September. From our perspective, it should have already done
so, given the economic and inflation data.
First, the current environment is completely different and significantly
more favorable than the one that prevailed at the time of the three
insurance rate cuts in late 2025. Undoing this easing alone is justified

today.
Second, the real GDP growth rate is well above its potential pace.
Our 3rd, 4th and 5th points are the following: unemployment is trending
downward, inflation is well above target, and there is a risk that infla-
tion expectations could become unanchored.
Sixth, the balance of risks between the two components of the Fed’s
mandate has shifted: the easing of employment concerns brings the
upward risks to inflation to the forefront.
Seventh, U.S. monetary policy is likely too accommodative — to a de -

gree difficult to assess precisely, given the inability to accurately esti -
mate the neutral rate (which, according to our estimates, falls within a
range of 3.25% to 4.25% in nominal terms).
2 See this Financial Times article: The Key Inflation Signal for Investors (August 19, 2026).
3 See this BIS paper for a recent overview of the debate: Energy Shocks and Inflation: Challenges for Monetary Policy, August 5, 2026.
4 65 months of core PCE inflation overshooting the 2% target.

But it is clear that current high productivity gains are pushing it higher.
A recalibration of monetary policy therefore appears necessary to bring
the Fed Funds target (currently 3.50–3.75%) at least toward the upper
end of our estimate of the neutral rate, or even into restrictive territory.
EIGHTH, THE FED MUST BACK UP ITS WORDS WITH ACTION
Regarding the outcome of the September 15–16 FOMC meeting, Ke -
vin Warsh has not shown willingness to act upon the latest data. He

wants a Fed that is less data-dependent and more guided by under -
lying trends. It is understandable and this shift could happen in the fu-
ture, following the conclusions of the task forces. However, aside from
the fact that Kevin Warsh is not the sole decision-maker, the latest
data for August points towards a rate hike. This is clear in light of the
strong rebound in nonfarm payroll employment. It is also clear regar -
ding inflation, as the monthly change (seasonally adjusted) in the core

CPI (+0.29%) exceeds the trigger threshold we had identified (a “big”
0.2%). In fact, the very persistence of high inflation4, combined with an
economic environment (both domestic and international) that remains
inflationary, argues for a rise in the Fed Funds target.
It would also be a matter of Kevin Warsh walking the talk to reinforce
the credibility of his remarks at Jackson Hole regarding the renewed
firmness of his commitment to ensure price stability. And it would also

be a matter of not adding to the tension and nervousness already pre-
sent in the bond markets, regardless of their origin. If the Fed takes the
markets’ signal into account — as Kevin Warsh seems inclined to do
when he considers that the rise in long-term rates is doing part of the
central bank’s job and when he highlights the reassuring anchoring of
their inflation expectations — then it must raise its policy rates without
further delay. Not only do economic conditions require it, but it is also

a matter of risk management.
Hélène Baudchon
helene.baudchon@bnpparibas.com
SOURCE: BLS, BEA, MACROBOND, BNP PARIBAS

► 聯準會9月升息1碼至3.75%-4.00%，最新點
陣圖顯示2026年仍有再升息一碼空間 ，2027
年傾向維持利率不變 ，2028年後才逐步回到
降息趨勢 。主席華許重申抑制通膨目標 ，升
息立場官員數明顯較前次會議增加 ，凸顯聯
準會鷹派政策態度 ，惟利率路徑較市場原先
預期的連續緊縮和緩，市場偏利多解讀。
► 聯準會 本次亦上修 2026-2027年GDP成長預
估，下修失業率預測 ，反映美國 經濟與勞動
市場尚未明顯轉弱；另一方面 ，能源價格上
升與成本轉嫁使整體及核心通膨預估再度上

修；景氣具韌性 、通膨回落速度偏慢 ，使聯
準會仍有條件維持較緊縮的政策立場 。在高
利率環 境可 能延 續 下，股 票評價 仍面 臨壓
力，配置上可相對偏向獲利與現金流穩定 、
防禦性較高的產業；債券則以中短天期 、高
信評企業債為主要方向 ，把握目前較高的收
益率。長天期公債仍受通膨 、財政紀律及期
限溢酬上升影響 ，宜待通膨與景氣出現更明
確降溫訊號後，再提高配置。

► 日本8月CPI年增1.9%，油價上漲帶來的外溢
影響，逐漸反應在消費成本上。7月勞工平均
薪資較去年同期成長 4.7%，創1997年1月以
來最大增幅；薪資增速追上通膨 ，帶動實質
薪資增長 。此外 ，第二季 GDP季增年率達
1.4%優於預期，8月製造業PMI因AI及半導體
需求暢旺上升，7月出口年增率高達23.2%，
創2022年10月來最快增速 。整體通膨升溫加
上景氣復甦，為日銀升息提供底氣。
► 日本央行會後 聲明稿表示：金融環境實質利
率偏低 ，將視情況調整貨幣政策 ，並強調升
息後金融環境仍維持寬鬆 ，未如市場預期的
鷹派，使日股持續上漲 、日圓回貶 。考量日
銀呵護 寬鬆金融環境 ，侷限日圓續強力 道，
但在美 國施 壓與 日 銀未來 仍可能 升息 背景
下，日圓難回到過去極弱 狀態。相較過去 ，
現在升值的日圓對日本出口 、科技股壓力可
控，日本10年公債殖利率來到3%，利率上升
有利 金融 股 ，投 資 短期或 以金融 、內 需為

主，科技、半導體股逢回可分批布局。
經濟與通膨升溫，日銀加快緊縮步調

熱門議題

### Page 7

投資焦點低波價值防禦
► 美、歐、日主要央行連袂升息 ，加上美伊衝
突惡化 ，國際油價高漲 ，通膨疑慮與成熟國
家債務壓力 ，使全球多數國家公債殖利率衝
高，市場擔憂高利率與高油價可能拖累經濟
成長，全球股債匯市承壓 、波動上升 ，並以
此前領漲多頭行情的晶片 、半導體和科技股
最明顯 。觀察今年以來 ，不止股市波動度上
升，債市的波動更不亞於 股票市場 ，統計過
去，當VIX恐慌指數與MOVE債券波動度走高
時，低波動或防禦股的表現 ，往往比成長股
更好，顯示當市場震盪加劇時 ，資金傾向轉
往穩健資產停泊。
► 最近一次聯準會升息 ，為2022 年因疫情爆
發、供應鏈中斷 ，導致通膨大幅上揚 ，迫使
央行採取緊縮貨幣政策 ，和目前通膨上升促
央行升息背景 類似。前次聯準會啟動升息後
數月，成長股 、費城半導體股表現受抑 ，防
禦股、價值股和低波動族群 ，在剛開始升息
的環境下 ，表現相對抗跌 。考量目前聯準 會
籠罩在升息陰霾下，可增加相關類股配置。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 最近一次聯準會升息後，防禦低波高息和價值股突出美國股債波動度上升時，低波動、防禦股表現較佳

金融市場波動上升，增配低波、防禦價值股更穩健

### Page 8

8
► 觀察最近一季 ，資訊科技 、通訊服務與非核
心消費等High Beta類股，表現明顯落後於大
盤；反觀價值股 、防禦股和低波動等因子 ，
甚至核 心消 費與 高 股利等 傳統類 防禦 性產
業，報酬相對較 穩健，受資金青睞 。顯示在
經歷過 上半 年科 技 、半導 體股的 大漲 行情
後，市場預期高 利率與地緣政治 風險可能維
持更久時間 ，重新聚焦在獲利持久、現金易
落袋為安的防禦型資產，更能持盈保泰。
► 從預估本 益比看，目前金融僅 16倍、防禦股
17.5倍、價值股 (以IWD ETF代表)17.9倍、
低波動 (以USMV ETF代表)19.6倍，皆低於
標普500指數 現在 的21.3倍，近月資金流向
物美價廉的低波動價值 、防禦族群，評價越
低、漲幅越高 。展望後市，AI長線基本面仍
持續看好 ，股市回檔或為評價 修正而 非盈餘
下滑，市場波動度提升之際 ，建議不過度 集
中於單一 產業或 個股，多元配置適度增加 低
波動 、 防禦 和價 值股，分 批布局 以控管 風

險，料將更能在震盪加劇時安穩求利。

資金輪動，關注波動度低和評價合理的防禦價值族群

### Page 9

9
股市
 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，利率創高添股市波動風險 ，加上成熟國家債務攀升 ，科技巨擘亦舉債
投入資本支出 ，未來獲利與變現 能力持續被市場檢視 ，金融市場震盪不減 。美伊衝突升溫，近期可
透過防禦、低波動或價值 股來降低風險；科技 及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利基本面佳 ，長線發
展可期，宜採逢回分批布局方式，分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，歐洲國防軍工
仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融等產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國 10年期公債利率將於 4.5%-5.0%
震盪，可逢利率走升時，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款
能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。

債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金融、
科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息後，年底前或明年初仍可能再度緊縮，日圓震盪加大偏升。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪加大偏升
商品
 沙烏地阿拉伯關閉輸油管線 ，加上荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張 ，能源供需失衡 ，油價年底前維持 偏高水位。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力 與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 298: OpenAI再推两款GPT-6模型：Sol、Luna定价腰斩，Astra能力加速下沉

Source PDF: all report pdf/OpenAI再推两款GPT-6模型：Sol、Luna定价腰斩，Astra能力加速下沉.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Astra 发布不到三周， GPT-6 模型扩容。 GPT-6 Sol 主攻复杂⼯作， GPT-6 Luna 瞄准⾼频
低成本任务。 OpenAI 称，两款新模型 API 价格较 GPT-5.6 的促销价低 50% ，均 “ 建⽴在
GPT-6 Astra 背后的技术进展之上 ” ，将 Astra 的优势带⼊更快、更实惠的模型，以⽀持规模
化⼯作，还针对缓存和推理效率进⾏优化。新模型尚未开放⾄ ChatGPT 普通聊天界⾯。和
Anthropic ⼀样， OpenAI 在呼吁放慢前沿 AI 开发的同时推出更具成本竞争⼒的模型。
摘要：
OpenAI 已经进⼀步扩充 GPT-6 产品线，并将部分 Astra 的能⼒带⼊更快、更便宜的模型。
美东时间 9 ⽉ 22 ⽇周⼆， OpenAI 宣布推出 GPT-6 Sol 和 GPT-6 Luna 两款新模型。距离 OpenAI 发
布其号称 “ 全球最智能 ” 的旗舰模型 GPT-6 Astra 还不到三周， OpenAI 已经进⼀步补⻬ GPT-6 产品

线，并将部分 Astra 的能⼒带⼊更快、更便宜的模型。
此次两款模型的另⼀个核⼼看点是价格： OpenAI 称， GPT-6 Sol 和 GPT-6 Luna 的 API 价格较
GPT-5.6 的促销定价还低 50% 。 OpenAI 称，这是由于其进⼀步提升了缓存和推理效率，并将节
省的成本直接让利给⽤⼾。
两款模型的定位也有所区分： Sol 更偏向⾼能⼒的专业⼯作、编码和智能体任务， Luna 则更强调
速度、成本和⾼频使⽤。 OpenAI 表⽰，两款模型都继承了 GPT-6 Astra 在专业⼯作、事实准确
性、编码、计算机使⽤和对⻬⽅⾯的技术进展。
根据 OpenAI 公告， Sol 和 Luna 即⽇起⾯向 Plus 、 Pro 、 Business 、 Enterprise 及 Edu ⽤⼾，在
ChatGPT Work 和 Codex 中提供，同时开放 API ；免费版和 Go 版⽤⼾则可以在桌⾯端应⽤中体验
GPT-6 Luna 。不过，两款模型⽬前尚未开放⾄ ChatGPT 普通聊天界⾯。

Astra 发布不到三周， OpenAI 再添两款 GPT-6
从 GPT-6 的发布节奏看， OpenAI 此次动作相当密集。
9 ⽉ 3 ⽇， OpenAI 正式推出 GPT-6 Astra ，并称其在计算机和浏览器操作、软件⼯程、⽹络安
全、科学和专业⼯作等领域达到新的能⼒前沿。 OpenAI 当时将 Astra 定位为 GPT-6 家族的旗舰模
型。
仅仅不到三周， OpenAI 就再次推出 Sol 和 Luna 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

OpenAI 的官⽅账号在社交媒体表⽰， Sol 和 Luna“ 建⽴在 GPT-6 Astra 背后的技术进展之上 ” ，将
Astra 的诸多优势带⼊ “ 更快速、更实惠的模型 ” ，以⽀持规模化⼯作。
这⼀产品节奏显⽰， OpenAI 并没有将 GPT-6 定位成⼀次只有旗舰模型的单点升级，⽽是在 Astra
发布后迅速向不同性能、成本和使⽤场景铺开产品线。
这也与此前 AI 模型⼚商越来越明显的产品策略相呼应：旗舰模型负责突破能⼒上限，⽽更快、更
便宜的版本则负责进⼊更多⾼频⼯作流。
从 Astra 到 Sol 、 Luna ，能⼒向更⼤规模场景下沉
与 Astra 强调前沿能⼒不同， Sol 和 Luna 的定位更加突出速度、成本和规模化使⽤。
OpenAI 称， Astra 仍然是其 “ 各⽅⾯表现最佳 ” 的模型，适合最困难、最重要的项⽬；⽽ Sol 和
Luna 则是把 Astra 背后的技术进展带⼊不同成本和使⽤规模的产品。
OpenAI 表⽰， Sol 和 Luna 不仅继承 Astra 的技术进展，还针对缓存和推理效率进⾏了优化。公司

称，这些优化能够降低模型运⾏成本，并最终通过更低的 API 价格反馈给⽤⼾。
OpenAI 还表⽰，两款模型在对⻬能⼒⽅⾯同样吸收了 Astra 的进展，相较各⾃的 GPT-5.6 版本有
所提升。
换句话说， OpenAI 此次并⾮单纯推出两个 “ 低价版 ” 模型，⽽是试图将 Astra 在能⼒和对⻬⽅⾯的
部分进步，向更⾼频、更⼤规模的模型调⽤场景延伸。
对于开发者和企业⽽⾔，这意味着此前只有在⾼价值任务中才值得调⽤的先进模型能⼒，未来
可能以更低的单位成本进⼊编码、智能体和企业⾃动化等⼤量重复性⼯作。
API 价格较 GPT-5.6 促销价直接腰斩
价格是此次发布最明确的商业信号。
OpenAI 宣布， GPT-6 Sol 和 GPT-6 Luna 的 API 价格，较 GPT-5.6 的促销价格下调 50% 。
⽬前 OpenAI 公布的 GPT-5.6 Sol 促销价格为每 100 万 Token 输⼊ 4 美元、输出 20 美元；因此， Sol
和 Luna 此次的价格下调意味着其 API 成本进⼀步向下。 OpenAI 的企业价格表也显⽰， GPT-5.6

Sol ⽬前仍处于促销定价阶段。
OpenAI 解释称，价格下降主要来⾃更⾼效的缓存和推理，⽽不是单纯的商业让利。
OpenAI 官⽅账号称， “ 更⾼的使⽤额度和更低的成本 ” 将给⽤⼾更多灵活性和迭代空间。

### Page 3

对于需要⼤量调⽤模型的开发者和企业来说，单位 Token 成本下降意味着同样的预算可以运⾏更
多任务，也意味着过去因成本⽽被限制的智能体⼯作流，有望获得更⼤的运⾏空间。
这也让 AI 模型的竞争进⼀步从 “ 谁的模型能⼒更强 ” ，延伸到谁能以更低成本提供⾜够强的模型能
⼒。
先进⼊ Work 和 Codex ，免费⽤⼾可体验 Luna
在产品覆盖上， OpenAI 此次也没有⽴即让 Sol 和 Luna 全⾯进⼊ ChatGPT 普通聊天界⾯。
根据 OpenAI 的安排， Plus 、 Pro 、 Business 、 Enterprise 及 Edu ⽤⼾可以在 ChatGPT Work 和
Codex 中使⽤两款模型，同时通过 API 调⽤。
免费版和 Go 版⽤⼾则可以在桌⾯端应⽤中体验 GPT-6 Luna 。
这⼀安排使两款模型的⾸发场景明显偏向⼯作和开发，⽽不是单纯⾯向消费者聊天。
其中， Sol 和 Luna 的低成本、⾼使⽤效率，也更适合需要频繁调⽤模型的编码任务、智能体⼯作

流以及企业内部⾃动化。
相⽐之下， GPT-6 Astra 仍然承担旗舰模型的⻆⾊。 Astra 发布后， OpenAI 甚⾄因需求激增⼀度
暂停新的 Pro 订阅注册，并称 Astra 需求 “ 前所未有 ” 。
因此，此次推出 Sol 和 Luna ，也可能有助于 OpenAI 将部分⾼频需求导向成本更低的模型，从⽽
在扩⼤使⽤规模的同时降低基础设施压⼒。
Sol 主攻复杂⼯作， Luna 瞄准⾼频低成本任务
从 OpenAI 公布的测试结果看，两款模型并⾮简单的 “ 同⼀模型不同价格 ” 。
Sol 承担的是更⾼能⼒需求的任务。 OpenAI 称， GPT-6 Sol 可以处理复杂的专业⼯作，同时凭借
更⾼的使⽤额度和更低的成本，让⽤⼾能够进⾏更多轮迭代。

### Page 4

在 AutomationBench 这⼀测试企业跨应⽤⼯作流的基准中， GPT-6 Sol 在 xhigh effort 下获得
33.2% ，⾼于 GPT-6 Astra 在 low effort 下的 30.3% ，也⾼于 Claude Opus 5 max effort 下的
26.9% ； OpenAI 称， Sol 完成每项任务的成本仅为 0.27 美元。
值得注意的是，⽆代码 AI ⾃动化平台 Zapier 也公布了对 GPT-6 Sol 的测试结果，得分同样为
33.2% 。
Zapier 称，其测试基于真实⼯作流，在 xhigh effort 下， GPT-6 Sol 的 33.2% ⾼于 GPT-5.6 Sol 的
28.77% ；同时， Sol 的 API 输⼊价格为 2 美元 / 百万 Token ，约为 GPT-5.6 Sol 的⼀半。
从具体任务来看， Zapier 称 GPT-6 Sol 尤其擅⻓运营类⼯作，在其测试中的得分达到 58% 。
Zapier 因此建议，正在使⽤ GPT-5.6 Sol 的⼯作流可以考虑升级到新模型，并强调 Sol 同时具备更

低价格和更⾼测试成绩。需要指出的是，这属于 Zapier ⾃⾝的测试和评价，并⾮对所有企业⼯作
流表现的独⽴结论。
在专业⼯作测试 Agents' Last Exam 中， GPT-6 Sol 最⾼获得 56.4% 的成绩；在 DeepSWE v1.1 软
件⼯程测试中， Sol 最⾼获得 68.8% ，与 Claude Fable 5 的最⾼成绩 69.9% 相差 1.1 个百分点，但
OpenAI 称其单项任务成本约低 80% 。
Luna 则更突出成本效率。
OpenAI 称，在 AutomationBench ⾼强度测试中， GPT-6 Luna 较 GPT-5.6 Luna 提⾼ 5.4 个百分
点，同时单项任务成本降低 58% ；在 DeepSWE v1.1 中， Luna 最⾼获得 66.6% ，与 Claude Opus
5 和 Fable 5 的中等强度表现相当，但每项任务成本分别低 93% 和 96% 。
此外， OpenAI 表⽰，两款模型的事实准确性也有所提升。其内部事实性测试显⽰， GPT-6 Sol

的错误数量约为前代⼀半； Luna 在更⾼推理强度下，也可以达到 GPT-5.6 Sol 的⽔平，⽽成本约
为后者的百分之⼀。需要注意的是，这⼀测试基于⽤⼾此前标记为事实错误的匿名 ChatGPT 对
话，并不代表所有真实使⽤场景。
AI 模型竞争从 “ 拼旗舰 ” 进⼊ “ 拼规模 ”
OpenAI 给出的价格表显⽰， GPT-6 Sol 输⼊价格从 GPT-5.6 Sol 促销价的 4 美元降⾄ 2 美元 / 百万
Token ，输出价格从 20 美元降⾄ 10 美元； Luna 输⼊价格从 0.20 美元降⾄ 0.10 美元，输出价格从
1.20 美元降⾄ 0.50 美元。
这意味着两款模型的 API 成本并⾮⼩幅下降，⽽是直接打五折。
OpenAI 解释称，更低的价格来⾃缓存和推理效率的提升。公司同时进⼀步优化了提⽰词缓存，
使智能体和⻓对话能够更多复⽤此前已经处理过的上下⽂。
对于开发者和企业客⼾⽽⾔，模型价格下降的意义不仅是 “ 每次调⽤更便宜 ” ，还意味着同样预算
可以承载更多轮模型调⽤。

尤其是编码智能体和企业⾃动化⼯作流需要⻓时间、反复调⽤模型，模型单位成本正在成为决
定智能体能否真正规模化部署的重要变量。
⼀边呼吁 “ 慢下来 ” ，⼀边把模型变得更便宜
值得注意的是， GPT-6 Sol 和 Luna 发布之际， OpenAI 与 Anthropic 实际上都在讨论 “AI 开发是否应
该放慢 ” 。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
本⽉早些时候， Anthropic CEO Dario Amodei 公开呼吁放慢前沿 AI 发展的速度，并警告快速推
进可能带来⻛险。 OpenAI CEO Sam Altman 随后表⽰认同⾏业需要放慢前沿模型发展的速度、
加强安全措施。 OpenAI ⾸席科学家 Jakub Pachocki 也曾提出，通过协调来放慢未来 AI 开发是确
保⾃我改进型 AI 安全的重要路径。
但与此同时， OpenAI ⾃⼰的产品迭代并没有放慢。
OpenAI 在发布 GPT-6 Astra 不到三周后⼜推出 Sol 和 Luna ，⽽且此次不仅增加模型选择，还将
API 价格直接降低 50% 。
同在本周⼆， Anthropic 也推出新模型 Claude Opus 5.5 ，并强调更低的运⾏成本。

两家头部 AI 公司同⼀天推出更具成本竞争⼒的模型，显⽰ AI ⾏业的竞争正在从单纯争夺旗舰模型
能⼒，进⼀步转向性能、速度与成本的综合竞争。
对于 OpenAI ⽽⾔， Astra 负责树⽴ GPT-6 的能⼒上限， Sol 和 Luna 则负责把这些能⼒以更低成本
推向更⼴泛的⼯作场景。
⼀边讨论如何放慢 AI 发展的速度，⼀边加快模型迭代、扩⼤使⽤范围并压低调⽤成本， AI ⾏业正
在进⼊能⼒与成本同时加速下探的新阶段。

• Data centres, power, compute and private investment-grade financing is further
diversifying private capital from buyouts.
• But the risk of AI disruption, overbuild, and capital misallocation is a frequent risk

discussed by large, listed private capital managers
• Our spotlight section includes an analysis of AI-related overfinancing risk
• Still, the macro environment is now the top point of concern amongst PC managers, even
more so than the exit environment
• Private capital growth remains strong but is becoming increasingly K-shaped. Scale,
investment performance, origination capabilities and product breadth are driving
fundraising toward the largest platforms, while weaker managers and less differentiated

products face pressure.
• Fundraising, AUM and fee-related earnings generally reached record or near-record
levels, despite weak sponsor M&A, uneven exits and disruption in parts of private wealth
and software.
• There has also been a drop in the number of articles on private credit in the media,
especially when it comes to “crisis”-mentioning variety. With public BDCs being at the
centre of this year’s rout, the drop in such trackable indicators likely bodes well for

sentiment and thus flows.
• The overall tone of Q2 results was strongly positive and confident, especially on
fundraising and structural growth. However, management teams were more cautious on
the timing of monetisations, private-wealth recovery, AI capacity risks and software
disruption.

### Page 7

Luke Templeman | Galina Pozdnyakova | Private capital monitor: AI is the 2nd biggest risk  |  September 2026
6
Key themes from Q2 earnings calls
Large, listed managers talk AI, wealth, fundraising, exits and credit
Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank.
1. AI infrastructure has become a firm-wide capital deployment and earnings theme, extending beyond data centres into power, compute, chips, connectivity and investment-
grade financing. Power availability is emerging as a more important constraint than capital. Although concentrated exposure to AI infrastructure is a risk if demand

expectations change, managers stressed contracted revenues, strong counterparties and hard-asset collateral as mitigants.
• Blackstone’s data-centre platform reached $185bn of total value, including projects under construction, up from $130bn at the start of 2026. Management said the
platform could double over the next few years and that the market for long-term ownership of stabilised data centres could eventually reach $1tn. The firm also said
nine of its ten largest Q2 mark-ups were AI-related.

• Apollo cited more than $8tn of cumulative AI-infrastructure investment through the cycle and led a $35bn Broadcom financing, described as the largest private-credit
financing to date.
• Brookfield estimated $10tn of AI-related capex across energy, data centres, compute and adjacent infrastructure. The firm said AI-related deployment was probably
running at approximately twice its normal 15%-20% business growth rate.
• Blue Owl reported more than 140 data centres owned or under construction and 15.3GW of leased and owned capacity

2. Private wealth remains strategically important, but private credit experienced a confidence and liquidity test. Early Q3 data suggests stabilising fundraising. The better wealth
outcomes increasingly came from private equity, infrastructure, real estate and multi-asset products rather than traditional private credit. Earnings calls suggest some
international family offices and smaller institutions behaved more cyclically than core US individual investors.

3. Record fundraising despite a "noisy" backdrop. Fundraising is concentrating with scaled, diversified platforms.
4. Realisations are recovering, but the market is highly selective (see pages 17-21 of this report)
5. Credit is broadening beyond sponsor-backed direct lending and real assets, infrastructure, defence and secondaries are benefiting from secular demand
6. Earnings are shifting toward recurring fees, transaction revenues and fee-related performance revenues. Firms are becoming more effective at capturing economics from

origination, financing and perpetual vehicles amidst a strategic shift from simply managing third-party capital toward monetising the broader platform through origination,
syndication, financing and advisory services.

### Page 8

Luke Templeman | Galina Pozdnyakova | Private capital monitor: AI is the 2nd biggest risk  |  September 2026
7
Key risks from Q2 earnings calls
Aside from AI, concerns are rising about rates and credit
Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank.
1. AI overbuild, capital misallocation and execution amongst most consistently voiced risks
• Brookfield said concerns about excess infrastructure capacity were reasonable and that, as in railways and fibre, some capita l would inevitably be poorly allocated.

• Blackstone acknowledged the potential for excessive exuberance, despite its conviction that power, chips and entitlements con strain supply.
• Both firms also identified scarce inputs and complex development requirements as execution bottlenecks.
2. AI disruption to software and white-collar services
• Blackstone said software, professional services and information services were experiencing weaker buyer interest despite many companies continuing to perform operationally.

• TPG reported mid-teens software bookings growth and said over 75% of its software exposure was well positioned to benefit from AI.
• Blue Owl said it had gained increasing comfort around software transition risk but continued to recognise it as a valid issue
3. Wealth-channel redemptions. Management frames this as narrative-driven, but a slower-than-expected sentiment recovery is a risk to retail flows. Managers emphasised that requests were
concentrated among a minority of investors concentrated in non-US family offices and smaller institutions and that underlying portfolio performance remained sound.

• The recovery appears to be under way but remains gradual. Blue Owl explicitly rejected a V -shaped recovery assumption, while Ares estimated that its BDC could return to flow stasis over the
next two to three quarters if current trends continued.
4. Realisation timing and reliance on capital markets. Managers stressed that timing is difficult to predict.
• TPG expects transaction and monitoring fees to step down in Q3 after pulling forward some transactions into Q2. Carlyle simil arly said Q2 capital-markets activity was unlikely to repeat in Q3

or Q4. Blackstone expects lower Q3 realisations before a stronger Q4 and 2027.
5. Credit-cycle normalisation and growing dispersion remain risks, although managers generally reported sound portfolio fundamental s in Q2.
6. Insurance competition, spread compression and regulation
• KKR said competition had increased and it was allocating somewhat less capital to insurance at current return levels. Managem ent described sector ROEs as structurally low in the current

competitive environment. Apollo cited 36 asset-manager entrants into retail annuities and argued that some competitors lack matu re origination, liability-generation and operating
capabilities. Apollo also highlighted offshore regulatory arbitrage and pushed for “equal capital for equal risk”, warning th at weaker regulatory structures could undermine industry trust.
7. Rates, inflation, energy and geopolitics. The macro backdrop remained a recurring source of uncertainty:

• Carlyle cited war in the Middle East, pressure on energy markets, stubborn inflation and AI -related public-market volatility.
• TPG said geopolitical uncertainty and changing rate expectations had reduced private -equity activity.
• Blackstone said higher rates were delaying the real -estate recovery and had pushed some exits out.

### Page 10

Luke Templeman | Galina Pozdnyakova | Private capital monitor: AI is the 2nd biggest risk  |  September 2026
9
Management comments – a few records in Q2 and a confident tone
Source: Company reports, Deutsche Bank.
• Stephen Schwarzman of Blackstone: “Blackstone delivered an outstanding second quarter, highlighted by strong growth in earnings and nearly $70 billion of inflows. Our
decision to lean into the artificial intelligence megatrend is leading to standout investment performance across numerous strategies and creating extraordinary opportunities for

growth. We’ve become a trusted partner at scale to many of the key innovators in this ecosystem, which positions our firm extremely well for the future.”
• Joseph Bae and Scott Nuttall of KKR: “Our second quarter results reflect the strength and breadth of KKR's global franchise. We reported record Fee Related Earnings, Total
Operating Earnings and Adjusted Net Income per share on both a quarterly and trailing twelve-month basis. Our continued return of capital to clients led to our strongest

monetization quarter ever and helped drive record new capital inflows over the past 12 months.”
• Marc Rowan of Apollo: “Our strong second quarter results reflect record earnings across Asset Management and Retirement Services, highlighting the quality and growing
scale of our business. We are at the forefront of modernizing how private markets operate by enhancing transparency, improving liquidity, and broadening access. In a market
evolving quickly with increasing demand for capital, the breadth of our origination capabilities combined with a principal mindset positions us to help shape what comes next.”

• Harvey Schwartz of Carlyle: “The second quarter was one of Carlyle’s strongest quarters in recent years, underscoring the power of our diversified platform. We delivered record
Fee Related Earnings, our highest Distributable Earnings in nearly four years, alongside strong fundraising, and exceptional realization activity. Carlyle continues to distinguish
itself as an industry leader and an outlier in returning capital to our clients, distributing nearly $7 billion during the quarter and $37 billion over the past year. …”

• Michael Arougheti of Ares: “We generated strong second-quarter results, including another record quarter of fundraising with more than $36 billion of inflows as our clients
continue to reward us due to our strong and consistent fund performance across our strategies. Our diverse global originationplatform enabled us to remain active investing in
attractive opportunities across the platform in a slower transaction environment and we are now seeing a meaningful pickup inour firmwide investment pipeline.”

• Jon Winkelried of TPG: “TPG delivered record results during the first half of 2026, reflecting the strength of our diversified and growing franchise…As clients increasingly
partner with TPG for access to our differentiated offerings across strategies, we continue to see significant opportunities to further expand our platform and build long-term
value for our shareholders.“
• Doug Ostrover and Marc Lipschultz of Blue Owl: “Blue Owl's ongoing growth in the second quarter reflects the strong investment performance we continue to generate across

strategies and the diversification of our business across platforms and geographies… The results we reported this morning continue to demonstrate the resilience of our
business and highlight our positioning as a key provider of scaled capital solutions in this evolving market landscape."

### Page 18

Luke Templeman | Galina Pozdnyakova | Private capital monitor: AI is the 2nd biggest risk  |  September 2026
17
Realisations 1/5
Reopening IPO markets, AI-related corporate demand and mature portfolios are all catalysts. But timing remains
unpredictable and exposed to geopolitics, public-market volatility, rates and AI-related valuation uncertainty.
Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Haver Analytics, Deutsche Bank.
1. KKR described Q2 as the largest monetisation quarter in its history, with realised

performance income of $848m and realised investment income of $220m.
Remaining unrealised gains were still approximately $18bn.
2. Carlyle returned nearly $7bn to investors in Q2 and $37bn over the prior year. Its
US buyout strategy returned 23% of fair value over 12 months, which
management said was more than twice the current industry average and above
the 20% long-term industry average.
3. TPG generated $5bn of Q2 realisations and almost $14bn in H1, up 28% year over

year.
4. Blackstone outlined the following segmentation when it comes to investor
demand for assets:
• AI, power, electrification and energy assets: strong strategic, sponsor and
IPO demand
• AI-unaffected businesses: broadly available liquidity
• Software, professional services and information services: materially weaker
activity because of AI uncertainty.
The firm estimated the third category represents approximately 30%-40% of the
private-equity market.

5. TPG similarly noted that industry realisations were down about 46% quarter over
quarter, although its own H1 activity was higher. It expects its monetisation
cadence to accelerate toward year-end and into 2027. Brookfield expects
transaction volumes to build in H2, while Blackstone expects weaker Q3
realisations followed by a stronger Q4 and 2027.

Source: Westpac McDermott Miller
Index Index
Sept
2026:
89.5
Above 100: More New Zealanders are optimistic about
the economic environment than are pessimistic.
Below 100: More New Zealanders are pessimistic
about the economic environment than are optimistic.
Confidence springs back up, but there’s still a lot of nervousness

out there.
• Spring is here. The mornings are getting lighter. And in Auckland the CRL is
FINALLY open. Against that backdrop, consumers across the country are feeling
a bit less downbeat about the economic outlook. Even so, there is still a lot of
nervousness about economic conditions.
• Consumer confidence rose 9 points in our latest Westpac McDermott-Miller
survey of New Zealand households, taking it to a level of 89.5. That’s a sizeable
lift after confidence fell to a three-year low earlier in the year. But even with this

recent pickup, consumer confidence is still well below average levels (Note:
a level below 100 means that there are more households who are pessimistic
about economic conditions than those who are optimistic).
• Since our last survey in June, some of the pressure on households’ finances
has eased. Most notably petrol prices have fallen from the eyewatering highs
we saw earlier this year (though at the time of writing, the outlook for petrol
prices is looking worrying again). We’ve also seen ongoing strength in key export

industries, which has been boosting incomes and sentiment in many regions.
• But even with those factors supporting sentiment, households are still being
buffeted by some powerful financial headwinds and they remain cautious about
their spending.

% of households who said their

Dubai oil (USD/barrel)
Not a lot left in the tank.
• The big concern for households remains the pressure on their finances.
Even with a modest improvement since our last survey, the vast majority of

households continue to report that their financial position has deteriorated over
the past year. And households aren’t expecting an improvement over 2027, with
economic conditions expected to remain sluggish.
• The pressure on households finances is coming on several fronts, with fuel costs
still a key concern. Even though petrol prices have fallen since their spike in the
early part of the year, prices at the pump aren’t low, with 91 unleaded currently

averaging around $3.12/ltr around the country. And with global fuel prices
pushing higher again in recent days, the risks for local petrol prices are firmly to
the upside.
• But it’s not just fuel prices that are worrying households. The past year has also
seen large increases in other living costs, along with continued softness in the
labour market.
• Importantly, the strength in inflation has seen the RBNZ hiking the Official Cash
Rate twice since July, and we expect further increases over the months ahead.

That’s seen upward pressure on mortgage interest rates.

Pumping the brakes.
 • With living costs and interest rates pushing higher, households are still very 
cautious about pulling out their credit cards.
 • The majority of households we spoke to told us they’ve cut back their 
spending on dining out and other entertainment activities. Households are 
similarly cautious about purchasing major items for the home, like appliances 
or furnishings.
 • But it’s not just discretionary purchases that households have become more 
cautious abouts since oil prices have spiked. In our separate survey in July, 
around 30% of households we spoke to said they’ve adjusted their spending on 
essentials like groceries, purchasing fewer or cheaper items. Many households 
also told us they’ve been driving less.

Index Index
Wellington and Auckland continuing to lag rural regions.
• While still below average, confidence has taken a step higher in nearly every

part of the country. That’s consistent with lower fuel prices putting money back
into households’ wallets right across the nation.
• The only exception to this pattern was in Taranaki/Manawatu-Whanganui, where
confidence was essentially unchanged this quarter and still very low. Regions in
the central and lower North Island are seeing some mixed economic conditions,
with firmness in sectors like agriculture, but broader softness in the labour
market and house prices.

• Even with confidence picking up across most of the country this quarter, there
are still big differences in economic conditions across regions. Those regions
with strong rural backbones are continuing to outperform the major urban
centres of Auckland and Wellington. In addition, labour market conditions have
been notably firmer in the lower South Island than elsewhere.

TECHNICAL
DETAILS
Survey description.
The Westpac-McDermott Miller Consumer Confidence Index summarises the net balance of optimistic/pessimistic responses to five questions: how
households’ financial situation has changed over the past year; whether now is a good time to buy a major household item; how households expect their

financial situation to change over the coming year; and near term and longer-term prospects for the New Zealand economy as a whole.
The first two of these questions are summarised in the Present Conditions Index, and the last three are summarised in the Expected Conditions Index. An
index number over 100 indicates that optimists outnumber pessimists, though the series may be above or below 100 on average. The survey also includes
questions on respondents’ spending on entertainment and eating out, and on what they would do with a $10,000 windfall.

Survey interviews were conducted over the period 1-12 September 2026. The sample size was 1,550.

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《美日聯合干預日圓走勢，治標不治本》
7月31日日本與美國聯合干預日圓走勢，主要基於兩
大動機：其一，若日圓崩跌或套利交易失序平倉，將
對全球金融市場造成衝擊；其二，如日本單獨干預日
圓，勢必得大量拋售持有美債，反而會推升美債殖利
率、擾動美國公債市場，形成「保日圓、傷美債」。
為此 ，美日聯合干預手法為：美國首先設立 FIMA
Repo機制，讓外國央行得以美債作為抵押品向聯準
會借入美元，而不必直接出售美債，既取得干預所需
流動性又緩解美債賣壓。其次在操作面上採取「賣歐
元、買日圓」的方式，避免美元自身淪為干預標的而
引發美元指數劇烈波動，同時間接支撐日圓、分散市
場壓力，防止美元與整體金融市場出現動盪。
不過就日圓本身而言，美國提供的政策預期雖使日圓

不致失序貶值、短期下探風險獲得抑制，但由於日本
財政持續擴張、殖利率上行壓力未解的結構性矛盾並
未改變，日圓仍難以就此重回明確的升值趨勢，本次
干預更像是「止血」而非「治本」，其後續走向終究
取決於日本貨幣政策與財政紀律的根本調整。
重點摘要

《匯率壓力凌駕基本面，日央陷提前升息困局》
8月17日，日本公布2026年第二季GDP成長率，實質
GDP季增0.3%、年化季增率1.1%，雙雙低於市場預
期的0.5%與2.0%。更值得關注的是成長結構表現不
佳，呈現「外需一枝獨秀、內需疲弱」的現象。第二
季 民 間 消 費 持 平、 零成長 ， 遠 低 於 市 場 預 期 的
0.5%；企業資本支出更呈現季減1%的衰退，顯示內
需成長動能仍顯不足。相較之下，經濟主要仰賴出口
成長 1.8% ， 再 加 上 高 市 內 閣 政 府 支 出 大 幅 增 加
1.3%，才使第二季經濟維持擴張而未陷入衰退。
通膨方面，7月日本核心CPI年增率升至1.9%，但仍
為連續第7個月低於日本央行2%的目標水準。此次回

升主要受到基期因素影響，整體而言仍大致符合市場
預期。因此，本行認為，日本目前經濟結構面臨內需
不確定性偏高的挑戰，而通膨壓力尚未達到迫使央行
加速緊縮的程度，加上日本央行甫於6月升息，年內
仍具備暫停升息並觀察經濟發展的空間。
然而，日圓持續承受貶值壓力，卻使日本央行再次升
息的可能性明顯升高。由利率期貨隱含機率觀察，自
美日於7月底聯手干預匯市後 ，市場對日本央行於 9
月或10月會議再度升息的預期持續上升。

力可控，年內仍具備暫停升息
並觀察經濟發展的空間。

更值得注意的是，過去傾向支持寬鬆貨幣政策的日本
首相高市早苗，近期傳出轉而支持貨幣政策正常化，
使市場對9月升息的預期機率一度上升至70%以上。
同時，高盛亦將日本央行下次升息的預估時點 ，由
2027年1月提前至今年9月會議。因此，儘管本行基
於經濟基本面判斷，日本並不具備年內再度升息的充
分條件，且過去五次升息經驗亦顯示，升息未必能有
效支撐日圓升值，但在政治壓力升高，以及市場升息
共識逐步成形的情況下，若日本央行選擇按兵不動，
反而可能造成市場預期落空，引發鴿派解讀，進一步
加劇日圓短期貶值壓力。
摩根大通日本首席經濟學家Ayako Fujita 亦表示：
「Markets have largely priced in a September rate

hike, so delaying it could instead cause market
turmoil.」（若延後升息，可能引發市場動盪。）
基於上述考量，本行同步調整預測，認為日本央行於
9月或10月會議再次升息的可能性已明顯提高，而日
央顯然已面臨騎虎難下的困境，預期9月會議將提前
升息。

《債市再遭拋售，日央提前升息恐雪上加霜》
值得注意的是 ，隨著利率升至1.0%市場原先認為升
息循環接近尾聲 ，自去年下半年起日債買盤逐漸回
溫，外資罕見轉為淨買超。然5月日本政府擬推動規
模達370兆日圓的《日本成長戰略》後，市場對財政
可持續性的疑慮快速升溫。5~8月內外資合計賣超20
兆日圓日本公債，30年期公債殖利率上升近 40個基
點。隨著債務疑慮、升息預期及年底預算案擴大赤字
等因素交織，日本利率風險持續攀升，不僅將加劇日
股波動也可能透過利差交易平倉影響全球資本市場。
因此，若日本央行提前於9月升息，本行將下調第四
季日股評等至「中性」。待市場消化預算案、修憲案
等不確定因素，以及年底美國通膨回落、聯準會降息
預期重新升溫後，再擇機布局。

另一方面，面對9月升息已漸成市場共識的局面，日
本央行為降低債市衝擊，不排除同步調整購債計畫。
目前日銀規劃每季縮減每月購債規模4,000億日圓，
並於2027年第二季後維持每月2.1兆日圓水準。若提
前升息，日銀可能放緩縮表速度，甚至將固定購債額
度改為區間操作，以保留在市場波動擴大時增加購債
的彈性，抑制殖利率過快上升對債市造成的衝擊。
重點摘要
資料來源：日本央行，2026/8，中國信託商業銀行整理

《日本長期基本面穩健，但短線波動加大》
近期日股表現震盪，主因受到利率居高不下所致，其
一，高市首相推出370兆日元的經濟成長戰略，導致
市場對日本財政可持續性的擔憂提升；第二，美日貨
幣政策偏鷹預期升高 ，日圓在美日干預後再度貶破
160大關，如前述日本央行在9月被迫提前升息機率
高；而聯準會主席華許在傑克森霍爾會議中再度強調
捍衛2%通膨目標決心，市場鷹派解讀、升息機率再
度提高，推升美債殖利率帶動日債利率同步上揚。
綜上短線日本利匯率壓力較大，財政與貨幣政策不確
定性高，建議日股短線中性看待；惟長線多頭格局延
續，外需不墜下日本經濟基本面尚算穩健，出口與工
具機訂單、羈押訂單表現佳，反映在日銀短觀大型製
造業指數創2018年以來新高。基此，2026年企業獲

利表現持續上修至雙位數成長的 21%，2027年企業
獲利預估維持正成長。資金面部分，外資仍舊呈現淨
買入，顯示市場流動性未出現恐慌，預期日股短線波
動後維持正向發展，但擴張動能已明顯下滑。
重點摘要
• 日股長線基本面多頭格局延
續，但短線日本利匯率壓力
較大 ，財政與貨幣政策不確
定性偏高 ，建議短線日股轉
向中性看待。

《盤勢觀點-通膨與景氣拉鋸，澳洲股市表現平淡》
(8/26)澳洲公布7月CPI年增3.5%、月增1.0%，較6月
的3.8%放緩，但由於燃油價格在連跌三個月後急升
7.5%，整體略高於市場預期 ，顯示通膨壓力仍在 ，
利率期貨市場隱含澳洲央行9月再度升息機率略升至
三成左右，推升澳幣再度走升。
不過，即便CPI數據略高於本行先前預期，係因中東
地緣衝突再度升溫所致，使央行短期內難以完全排除
進一步緊縮的可能。不過，就景氣面而言，最新7月
份失業率由6月的4.4%微升至4.5%，但勞動市場已自
年初高點逐步鬆動，加以高利率與能源成本對需求面
的壓抑持續顯現，家庭消費與房市活動明顯轉弱。
而以目前政策利率4.35%、CPI 3.5%換算，實質利率

約落在0.85%，接近1%高位外，已明顯高於去年第
四季升息前的水準，顯示目前利率對景氣仍具足夠限
制性；因此，即便通膨年增率自高點回落緩慢，短期
內難以見到降息空間，但預估澳洲央行短期間亦沒有
升息的壓力，本行維持澳洲利率將在高檔停留更久的
預測不變，澳洲持續維持中性看法。
重點摘要
• 澳洲經濟基本面降溫趨勢明
顯，澳洲央行將持續暫停升
息；高利率壓力不利景氣復
甦，因此維持澳洲股市中性
評等。

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
市場投資展望
重點摘要
本月關鍵圖表
谷歌、臉書短期催化劑有限，指
數上行空間有限
分析師／張偉漢
研究範疇／通訊服務
市場投資展望
《谷歌、臉書短期催化劑有限 ，指數上行空間有限 》
通訊服務指數兩大權值股谷歌與臉書雖在 7 月底財
報時繳出強勁表現，但在 ROIC 持續回落時仍指出
2027 年資本支出仍將顯著成長仍為通訊指數帶來壓
力。而谷歌 8 月再次經歷高階人才出走、谷歌旗艦
模型與臉書 AI 投資變現遲未明朗更為指數上行增添
壓力。隨著資本支出仍將積極成長 ，市場在接下來
勢必以更嚴格角度檢視資本支出效率與變現進度 。
雖然本行認為谷歌 、臉書基本面短期仍具一定韌性 ，
臉書法律尾端風險大幅收斂 、谷歌輕量模型的變現

路徑持續拓寬 ，但觀察市場估值 ，顯示兩公司仍在
承壓。短期沒有新的催化劑下 ，指數上行動能仍較
有限。因此，本行對通訊服務指數的看法維持中立 。
• 繼 7 月流失 Gemini 大模型與
前沿技術的核心研發靈魂人物
後，谷歌高階技術人才出走潮
仍在繼續。
• 臉書 167 億美元和解兒少安全
與社群成癮訴訟，最大單一尾
部風險解除，然歐洲可能面臨
新的監管挑戰。
• 谷歌模型應用場景深化，但旗
艦模型進展仍持滯；臉書開源
與代理人雙軌並進，但變現時
程仍未明。
1
資產 3Q26 看法 投資展望
通訊服務 中立 通訊服務主成分谷歌與臉書短期承壓。谷歌流失 AI 人才，或已影響
到模型發布延後及研發進度放緩，臉書 AI 投資變現仍未明朗。在預
期 2027 資本支出仍將顯著成長的預期下，市場在接下來勢必以更嚴
格角度檢視資本支出效率與變現進度。短期催化劑不明限制兩權值股
的上行動能，也限制了指數的上行空間，維持評等為中立。
資料來源：Koyfin、Capital IQ，2026/08/31；中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/08/31。

《繼流失 Gemini 核心人才，谷歌又見大將離職》
谷歌首席科學家 Jeff Dean 宣布離職當日谷歌股價
重挫近 5%，反映市場對其重要性的認知。Dean 在
谷歌任職 27 年，是公司搜尋引擎誕生初期即參與核
心架構設計的元老級人物：他主導共同設計了奠定
谷歌雲端與資料處理能力的分散式系統，其後領導
團隊開發 TensorFlow，並在近年出任公司首席科學
家，長期被視為谷歌工程文化的代表人。與 Dean

同步離職的另三人同樣具備不可替代性：資深研究
員 Sanjay Ghemawat 與 Dean 資歷相當，長年密
切合作，被《連線》雜誌譽為「網路時代最重要的
軟體工程師之一」；Oriol Vinyals 現任 Google
DeepMind 研究副總裁，是 Gemini 模型家族的技
術共同負責人；Quoc Le 為 Google Brain 創始成員
之一，2014 年的研究奠定了現今大型語言模型訓練
範式的重要源頭。四人合計代表谷歌過去二十餘年
在分散式運算、深度學習與生成式 AI 三個世代的技
術資產核心，此次集體出走並共同創辦專注科學與
工程領域 AI 應用的新創公司 Discovery Loop，雖
被定調為「和平分手」，但仍是谷歌 AI 組織近年最
重大的人事變動之一，發生的時點又恰逢市場質疑
谷歌能否於前沿模型能力上維持對 OpenAI、
Anthropic 的競爭力，人才流失的訊號意義不容忽
視。雖然谷歌透過既有高階人才火線補位展現組織
韌性，但核心創辦一代技術骨幹的流失、加上前沿

AI 人才在新創與大型平台間的持續競逐，仍是中長
期須觀察的組織穩定性風險，也是本行認為谷歌相
對其他權值股增添的獨特變數。

理期間，市場傳聞求償金額上看 1.4 兆美元一度使

Document 399: インフレ警戒を強めるＥＣＢ ～来年以降も利上げを継続へ～

Source PDF: all report pdf/インフレ警戒を強めるＥＣＢ ～来年以降も利上げを継続へ～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
